# Founder preview baseline — 29 Oct 2026

Built from: not a design task (capture), `PRODUCT/APPROVED/BASELINE/CAPTURE_BRIEF_2026-10-29.md`.
These pictures are review inputs, not a LOCK record. App source commit: `d08b2d17884dd44b4c22676a61175ef8c0619871`.

Route (c): Flutter visual-test render at **430 × 932 logical, DPR 3; 1290 × 2796 PNG**. The native build driver is held
following its SwiftPM nested-sandbox refusal. No native build, install or bypass was attempted. The paired phone is now
available, but that does not clear the held build route. The iOS status bar is absent; its safe area is preserved.
Actual app headers and tabs remain where the shell shows them; leaf pages naturally omit the tab bar. Long pages use
the full phone viewport, with separately indexed scroll/chapter states. No stitching or cropping.

The harness reads the production `ZyraApp` theme and scroll behavior without mounting startup, authentication or lock
wrappers. It uses the iOS test target, real bundled Poppins/Syne and decoded offline sample portraits. The system San
Francisco font is loaded in memory only for missing glyph fallback (the verified check symbol); no font asset was copied.
The environment receipt records its hash. No member data, backend transport or app source was changed.

Picture states below were reachable at the original capture commit. The retirement note identifies historical
developer panels. A normal, empty, loading, error or held state with no separate reachable local control is
**not in preview** for that page; none was invented. Native camera, photo picker and keyboard
surfaces are outside this headless preview. Retired Saved/Paused destinations and unavailable role/page pairs are recorded
below. Notifications remains in the generated inventory but has no current opener; see the coverage notes.

## Tier 4 retirement — 29 Oct 2026

The original capture commit ff65027 contained 301 PNGs. After the selected designs were kept in OA-ZYRA-316 Tier 4
(source commit 9e903fb), 20 explicitly unchosen Circle bubble/control captures moved to
`~/_to_delete/OA-ZYRA-316_TIER4_TESTS_2026-10-29/`, with their original paths and hashes in the local move receipt.
This packet now has **281 PNGs**; every retained PNG was byte-identical at retirement. The later one-file recapture below replaces the blank invite. Coverage below is unchanged.
The 20 retained developer-panel captures document the pre-cleanup option lists; Tier 4 intentionally shortened those lists.
They are historical capture inputs. The 261 chosen page/state PNG comparisons match the post-cleanup render exactly.
Original capture evidence rows remain unchanged. No new capture, LOCK or image approval is implied.

## Sign-up and onboarding — 29 Oct 2026 (queue10)

Built from: not a design task; `../SIGNUP_CAPTURE_BRIEF_2026-10-29.md` (Founder-named capture). These829pictures are review inputs, not a LOCK.
Same route(c),430×932logical @3x,1290×2796PNG,actual production theme/fonts/scroll behavior,safe59top/34bottom,no native status bar. Native route remains HELD.
No app source changed: all320SOURCE/lib files retain their hashes. Offline only, HTTP denied, no real account, OAuth success, identity check, payment or publication.

Seeker01–16 covers entry and essentials;17MyZYRA is the Finish later branch. The optional Complete setup branch instead has17next steps/roadmap,18Profile introduction,19–40Profile/consent,41Bio stage,42–46Bio,47Compatibility stage,48–187Values/preferences,188Review stage,189Review and190currentMyZYRA.
Guardian01–19 covers the actual current signup sequence. Both final destinations are the current ordinary member shells in their existing local preview modes.
State names and How reached distinguish actual traversal from detached public presenters for loading/error. Existing DEV skips supply local unanswered-page fixtures only; they do not verify or publish anyone.
Long content uses overlapping phone viewports; no image is stitched or cropped. Male full-flow questions and current female-only wording/relationship differences are included.

Known existing limitations: the filled Bio switch emits the exact ListTile background/ink diagnostic; the capture records that diagnostic only within Bio and forwards every other Flutter error. The initial long-answer Lifestyle summary overflow was retained in failed-run evidence; final captures use shorter valid existing choices through actual controls. No product repair is included in this capture-only task. Native picker/camera/keyboard and service-only save/restore failures without public offline seams are unavailable here; detailed gap inventory and written refusals are in local SIGNUP_CAPTURE evidence.
529extended files were mechanically renumbered by+2, with hashes preserved, to include the two real setup introduction pages. Future captures emit those same ordinals.
